### [113] [Argus: A General-Purpose Agentic Runtime for Long-Horizon Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05144)
*Boxiu Li,Zimo Wen,Yijia Fan,Junxiang Lei,Sufeng Guo,Jiaao Wu,Ruize Tang,Mukai Li,Yifei Shen,Xiaoyu Chen,Wanbo Zhang,Runjing Gu,Yifei Gao,Yuheng Wu,Xuyao Huang,Zelong Zhao,Jiachen Zhang,Shibo Hu,Hangxi Guo,Yilin Chen,Yuzhe Zhang,Fan Yang,Chuan Wen,Xian Zhang,Xuanhe Zhou,Zhijie Deng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Argus是一个固定权重、自我演化的agentic运行时系统，通过Manager、Planner、Engineer和Reviewer四个角色在持久化项目状态上执行有界任务。它区分稳定用户意图与操作目标/约束/验证标准，仅在接受角色所有审核和任务原生验证后，才接纳记忆、技能、程序和验证器等。在七个GPT-5.5基准竞技场中，Argus在SWE-Bench Pro上达到约78%（对比Direct Copilot的59%），使用1.41倍的聚合token；验证门控的自我演化使成熟wave的解决输入token减少21%，活跃工作流时间减少15%，记录34次验证器恢复和22次严格审查循环救援。此外在AARRI-Bench上达到76.8%，数学数据合成上领先28.0分，并展示了上游合入的RWKV6内核、多日数学活动及254个任务的论文流水线成果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视野推理需要能够在证据支持当前方法时持久执行、在测量发现失败、隐藏约束或目标错误时转变方向的agentic运行时。现有系统缺乏在固定权重下通过运行时状态积累已验证知识并自我演化的能力。

Method: Argus采用四个角色（Manager、Planner、Engineer、Reviewer）的架构，在持久化项目状态上执行有界任务。系统分离稳定用户意图与操作目标/约束/验证标准，并采用角色所有审核加任务原生验证的准入机制，只接纳通过验证的记忆、技能、程序和路由决策。自我演化通过持久化运行时状态和控制策略实现，不修改模型权重，在操作者拥有的升级点之间自主执行。

Result: 在SWE-Bench Pro上达到约78%（Direct Copilot为59%），使用1.41倍聚合token；AARRI-Bench上76.8%；数学数据合成上28.0分差距；GPU内核和语言模型训练结果有竞争力。验证门控自我演化后，成熟SWE-Bench wave的solve-input token减少21%，活跃工作流时间减少15%，记录了34次验证器恢复和22次严格审查循环救援。实际应用中：RWKV6内核上游合入、数学活动保留被证伪路径并推进证明支持的边界更新、六个论文流水线完成254个任务经历16次阶段回滚。

Conclusion: 固定权重、自我演化的harness能够修正、恢复并积累经过验证的方法，同时生成结构化轨迹供未来的监督学习和强化学习使用，证明了不改变模型权重即可实现持续的运行时自我进化与性能提升。

Abstract: Long-horizon reasoning requires an agentic runtime that can persist when evidence supports its current approach and pivot when measurements reveal failure, hidden constraints, or a misspecified objective. We present Argus, a persistent, self-evolving runtime in which Manager, Planner, Engineer, and Reviewer execute bounded missions over durable project state. Argus separates stable user intent from operational objectives, constraints, and verification criteria, and admits memories, skills, procedures, verifiers, routing decisions, and rejected routes only after role-owned review and, when available, task-native verification. Model weights remain fixed; self-evolution occurs through persistent runtime state and control policy, with autonomous execution between operator-owned escalation points. Across seven GPT-5.5 benchmark arenas, Argus achieves about 78% on SWE-Bench Pro versus 59% for Direct Copilot while using 1.41 times the aggregate tokens. After verification-gated self-evolution, mature SWE-Bench waves use 21% fewer solve-input tokens and 15% less active workflow time per task than startup waves, while recording 34 verifier recoveries and 22 strict review-loop rescues. Argus also reaches 76.8% on AARRI-Bench and a 28.0-point gap on mathematical data synthesis, with competitive GPU-kernel and language-model-training results. Beyond benchmarks, an optimized RWKV6 kernel was merged upstream; a multi-day mathematics campaign retained falsified routes and proof-backed frontier updates; and six paper pipelines completed 254 missions with 16 stage rollbacks. These results show that a fixed-weight, self-evolving harness can revise, recover, and accumulate verified approaches while producing structured trajectories for future supervised and reinforcement learning.

</details>


### [114] [Similarity All The Way Up: Multilingual Generalization in LLMs Relies on Language-Level Similarity Structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22699)
*Supantho Rakshit,Adele Goldberg,Henry Conklin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究LLM在多语言泛化能力上的表现，发现LLM的潜在表征能够高度恢复印欧语系的层次相似性结构（即语言家族树），并且模型反映这种语言相似性结构的程度与其在XNLI多语言基准上的性能相关，表明相似性驱动的泛化机制在大规模模型中同样适用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在跨语言泛化方面表现不佳，尤其是在训练数据中匮乏的非英语语言上。作者希望理解为什么有些模型能比另一些更好地进行多语言泛化，并通过认知科学中"相似性驱动的泛化"这一经典理论框架来解释这一现象。

Method: 作者借鉴认知科学中关于相似性空间表征与泛化关系的长期研究，考察LLM的潜在表征是否能够捕捉不同语言之间的层次相似性结构。具体做法是检查LLM表征空间中语言家族树的恢复程度，并将这种结构相似性与模型在XNLI多语言自然语言推理基准上的表现进行相关性分析。

Result: LLM的潜在表征在很大程度上恢复了印欧语系的层次结构——同属一个子族的语言在表征空间中聚集紧密。此外，模型反映语言相似性结构的程度与其在XNLI上的性能之间存在相关性。

Conclusion: 这项工作将经典的相似性驱动泛化理论推广到大规模模型场景，表明能够将相似语言表示为相似分布的模型，从一个语言向另一个语言迁移泛化时表现更好。

Abstract: As Large Language Models (LLMs) grow more capable across diverse tasks, their (in)ability to generalize remains difficult to quantify and poorly understood beyond limited domains. In particular, LLMs are known to struggle generalizing multilingually, to languages outside of English, and that are poorly attested in their training data. To understand why this may be, and what enables some models to perform better than others, we turn to a long history of work across the cognitive sciences, arguing that successful generalization derives from appropriate representations in similarity space. We look at how well LLMs' representations capture the hierarchical similarity structure between distinct languages. Strikingly, we show LLMs' latent representations largely recover the hierarchical structure of the Indo-European language family tree -- grouping languages that are members of the same subfamily closely together in representation space. Furthermore, we show that the degree to which models reflect the similarity structure of languages correlates with their performance on XNLI, a multilingual natural language inference benchmark. This extends classic work on similarity-driven generalization at scale, showing how models that represent similar languages similarly generalize better from one language to another.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [115] [A Primal Perspective on Distributionally Robust Optimization: An Investigation on Modeling and Solution Strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04123)
*Yiqi Tian,Bo Zeng*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种从原始视角（primal perspective）出发的分布鲁棒优化（DRO）算法框架BiCS，通过直接处理模糊集中的分布并构造分布割（distribution cuts）来求解DRO问题，适用于标准DRO、几乎必然DRO、含机会约束的DRO及带局部信息增强模糊集的DRO，数值实验表明其在矩和Wasserstein模糊集上性能优越。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统单阶段DRO的对偶重构方法在模型或模糊集结构复杂时变得困难甚至不可行；现有紧致重构在某些场景下不可用或计算困难。因此需要一种从原始视角出发、对复杂模糊集结构更具适应性的求解方法。

Method: 提出BiCS算法框架，直接从原始视角处理闭的（可能无界的）样本空间上的模糊集中的分布，通过构造并利用分布割（distribution cuts）来迭代求解DRO问题。该框架不依赖对偶重构，能够灵活处理多种DRO变体，包括标准DRO、几乎必然DRO、各种机会约束DRO以及由局部信息增强的模糊集。

Result: 在矩模糊集和Wasserstein模糊集上的数值实验中，BiCS展现出优越性能，能够求解那些现有紧致重构不可用或计算困难的实例。局部信息研究还使最坏情况分布的变化直接可视化。

Conclusion: BiCS作为从原始视角出发的通用DRO求解框架，突破了传统对偶方法的局限性，在处理复杂模糊集结构和各类DRO变体时展现出更强的适用性和求解效率。

Abstract: As a popular optimization scheme, distributionally robust optimization (DRO) protects decisions against ambiguity in probability distributions. For (single-stage) DRO, prevailing dual reformulations can become difficult when model or ambiguity-set structures are complex. We study DRO from a primal perspective, working directly with distributions in ambiguity sets on closed, potentially unbounded sample spaces. This perspective leads to an algorithmic framework, referred to as BiCS, that constructs and leverages distribution cuts to achieve strong performance. We show that BiCS is applicable to standard DRO, almost-sure DRO, DRO with various chance constraints, and DRO with ambiguity sets strengthened by local information. Numerical experiments with moment and Wasserstein ambiguity sets show that this framework demonstrates superior performance, including solving cases where the examined compact reformulations are unavailable or computationally difficult. The local-information study also makes changes in worst-case distributions directly visible.

</details>


### [116] [Evacuation Planning for Disaster Preparedness: An Adaptive Robust Optimization Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04225)
*Jaehyuk Kim,Merve Bodur,Maria E. Mayorga,Osman Y. Ozaltin*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出首个自适应鲁棒疏散规划模型，联合优化避难所选址、疏散路线分配、救援物资预置及灾后物资分配，通过分区界定算法在保持可解性的同时最小化最坏情况下未满足救援需求和拥堵相关疏散时间的加权和，并量化了集中式路线规划相比用户自选路线的价值（最坏情况下未满足需求降低90.6%，疏散时间降低79.3%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灾害疏散规划需要在疏散人数及其空间分布未知的情况下做出关键决策（避难所选址、路线分配、物资预置），且这些决策高度相互依赖，需在最大化救援需求覆盖和最小化疏散时间之间平衡。现有模型未能联合优化这些相互关联的决策，缺乏统一的鲁棒优化框架。

Method: 构建自适应鲁棒疏散规划模型，同时优化避难所选址、疏散路线分配、救援物资预置及灾后物资分配四类决策；刻画含混合整数补偿的问题理论复杂度特征；设计分区界定（partition-and-bound）算法，通过仅对不确定性集合中最关键的子集进行选择性分区来保持可解性，同时生成强上下界；并构建用户自选路线的替代模型以量化集中式路线规划的增量价值。

Result: 计算实验表明，集中式路线规划相较分散式用户自选路线，在最坏情况下可使未满足需求和疏散时间分别降低90.6%和79.3%；适应性灾后物资再分配进一步改善救援需求覆盖；集中式路线规划主要通过协调跨避难所撤离人流来缓解拥堵，而适应性再分配主要在救援物资稀缺或预置不灵活时提升需求覆盖。

Conclusion: 疏散路线规划与救援物资分配的协调在不确定条件下创造了显著的运营价值。集中式路线规划与自适应物资分配在改善灾后响应效果方面具有互补作用，二者应协同使用以最大化应急响应的整体效能。

Abstract: Evacuation planning for disaster preparedness requires making critical decisions under uncertainty before the number and spatial distribution of evacuees are known, including shelter location, evacuation route assignment, and relief supply prepositioning. Because these decisions are highly interdependent, planners must balance the competing objectives of maximizing relief demand coverage and minimizing evacuation time. We propose, to our knowledge, the first adaptive robust evacuation planning model to jointly optimize shelter locations, evacuation route assignments, relief supply prepositioning, and post-disaster relief item distribution. The model minimizes the worst-case weighted sum of unmet demand for relief items across shelters and the congestion-dependent evacuation time. We characterize theoretical complexity drivers of the resulting problem with mixed-integer recourse and develop a partition-and-bound algorithm that maintains tractability by selectively partitioning only the most critical subpartition of the uncertainty set while producing strong upper and lower bounds. To quantify the value of centralized route planning, we also formulate a user route choice alternative in which evacuees choose among acceptable routes. Computational experiments quantify the value of centralized route planning, which reduces worst-case unmet demand and evacuation time by up to 90.6\% and 79.3\%, respectively, relative to decentralized user route choice. Adaptive post-disaster supply redistribution further improves relief demand coverage. Coordination between evacuation routing and relief distribution creates substantial operational value under uncertainty. Centralized route planning primarily mitigates congestion by coordinating evacuee flows across shelters, whereas adaptive redistribution primarily improves relief demand coverage when relief supplies are scarce or inflexibly prepositioned.

</details>


### [117] [Strong error analysis for the stochastic momentum optimizer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04245)
*Davide Gallon,Arnulf Jentzen*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文对动量SGD优化器进行了严格的误差分析，建立了其收敛速率与学习率大小、小批量大小以及单点凸性常数大小之间的定量关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SGD及其变体（如Adam、AdamW、MUON）是大规模AI系统训练中的核心优化方法，其中动量SGD是加速这些优化器的关键组件。然而，现有的分析大多针对标准SGD，缺乏对动量SGD严格的收敛性理论保证。

Method: 通过理论分析，建立了动量SGD优化器的收敛率，并将其与学习率（步长）、小批量大小以及单点凸性常数等关键超参数显式关联起来。

Result: 给出了动量SGD优化器的收敛速率，具体表现为学习率、小批量大小和单点凸性常数的函数。

Conclusion: 为动量SGD提供了严格的误差分析理论框架，从而为理解基于动量的优化器（如Adam、AdamW等）在实际AI训练中的加速行为提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Stochastic gradient descent (SGD) optimization schemes are the methods of choice for the optimization of deep neural networks (DNNs) in artificial intelligence (AI) systems. Often not the standard SGD method is used but instead suitable accelerated, adaptive, and/or normalized variants of standard SGD such as Adam, AdamW, and MUON are employed to train large scale AI systems in practically relevant settings. The acceleration (higher order convergence speed) in all these popular optimizers relies on the momentum SGD optimizer. In this work we provide a rigorous error analysis for the momentum SGD optimizer. In particular, we establish convergence rates for the momentum optimizer in terms of the size of the learning rate (step size), the size of the mini-batch, and the size of the one-point convexity constant.

</details>


### [118] [Dynamic Dispatching for Time-Sensitive Blood Sample Collection and Delivery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04275)
*Arash Dehghan,Aliaa Alnaggar,Mucahit Cevik,Merve Bodur*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究医院与诊断实验室中血液样本采集的实时车队调度问题。样本请求随时间随机到达各采集中心，调度者需要在硬性截止期限和车辆容量约束下，反复决定派送哪些车辆、访问哪些中心、是立即取件还是合并到后续行程。作者将问题建模为马尔可夫决策过程，提出一种基于神经近似动态规划的集中调度框架，采用双值函数分解（分别对车辆状态和采集中心状态建模）并通过对后决策状态的训练来学习价值估计，最终通过匹配模型选择调度动作。在大多伦多地区真实网络上的实验表明，该策略相比短视基线将按时交付样本比例提升1-9个百分点，在车队紧俏、容量受限、截止期限紧迫和路径受限时增益最大，并减少对外部快递的依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 静态规划模型在需求未知前固定路线，反应式启发式只响应当前积压，两者均未前瞻未来到达的样本请求。由于样本有短暂的活性窗口，延迟交付会导致昂贵的重新采集并延误诊断，因此需要一种能平衡"立即采集"与"集中等待后续行程"的实时调度方法，在决策时前瞻未来请求。

Method: 将问题建模为马尔可夫决策过程，提出神经近似动态规划框架。核心创新是双值函数分解：分别用神经网络表示车辆状态和采集中心状态的价值，在post-decision状态上训练。学习到的价值估计通过匹配公式整合，在供给可用性、需求紧迫性和下游机会成本之间权衡选择调度动作。

Result: 在基于大多伦多地区的真实网络计算实验中，与短视基线和消融变体对比，所提出的双值函数策略将按时交付的样本量比例提高1至9个百分点。增益在车队紧张、容量受限、截止期限紧迫和路径受限的情况下最大；按时交付的提升也减少了对昂贵外部快递服务的依赖。

Conclusion: 基于神经近似动态规划的双值函数分解方法能有效解决随机需求下的实时样本采集车队调度问题，通过前瞻未来请求显著提升交付及时性，尤其在系统受限严重时优势明显，同时降低运营成本。

Abstract: Hospitals and diagnostic laboratories rely on couriers to collect blood samples from geographically dispersed collection centres and deliver them for analysis before their short viability windows expire; late deliveries force costly re-collection and can delay diagnosis. We study the real-time dispatching of such a courier fleet, in which sample requests arrive stochastically at the centres throughout the day and a central dispatcher must repeatedly decide which vehicles to send, which centres each should visit, and whether to collect urgent samples immediately or consolidate them into later trips, subject to hard delivery deadlines and vehicle capacity limits. Unlike static planning models, which fix routes before demand is known, and reactive heuristics, which respond only to the current backlog, our approach anticipates future arrivals when weighing immediate collection against consolidation. We formulate the problem as a Markov decision process and develop a neural approximate dynamic programming framework for centralized dispatch. The method introduces a dual value function decomposition that separately represents vehicle states and collection-centre states through neural networks trained on post-decision states. These learned estimates are integrated through a matching formulation that selects dispatch actions while balancing supply availability, demand urgency, and downstream opportunity cost. Computational experiments on a realistic Greater Toronto Area network compare the proposed policy with myopic baselines and ablation variants. Results show that the proposed dual value function policy raises the share of sample volume delivered on time by 1 to 9 percentage points over myopic baselines, with the largest gains under tight fleet, capacity, deadline, and routing constraints; these on-time gains, in turn, reduce reliance on costly external couriers.

</details>


### [119] [Achieving First-Order Statistical Improvements in Data-Driven Optimization: From No-Free-Lunch to Amplified Decision Perturbation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04312)
*Henry Lam,Tianyu Wang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文对数据驱动优化方法进行了系统性剖析，提出了"方向扰动经验优化"（EO+）的统一框架，涵盖正则化、分布鲁棒优化、迁移学习和上下文优化等众多方法。研究发现：在没有正确指定的额外侧信息的条件下，任何EO+方法最多只能获得二阶改进（"没有免费午餐"）；但当利用几何上有效的侧信息时，通过选择比文献中典型建议更大的超参数，可以实现一阶改进，并基于超额风险估计提出了最大化一阶增益的原则性方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据驱动优化方法虽然在鲁棒性或正则化视角下直觉上合理，但其统计收益往往不明确，且缺乏系统性的理解。作者希望提供一个统一的框架来剖析这些方法的统计性能边界和适用条件。

Method: 提出"EO+"框架作为统御多种数据驱动优化方法的伞形概念，基于"方向扰动"视角进行理论分析。通过对超额风险的估计（利用系统知识或bootstrap重采样）来量化统计改进的阶数，并论证几何有效侧信息的重要性。

Result: 1）证明无正确指定侧信息时，任何EO+方法最多只能实现二阶改进（消极结论）；2）展示几何有效的侧信息配合显著更大的超参数可实现一阶改进（积极结论）；3）建立了与蒙特卡洛模拟中控制变量原理的联系，解释了为何几何有效侧信息是必要的。

Conclusion: 数据驱动优化的统计性能受限于可用侧信息的几何质量：没有正确的侧信息，"无免费午餐"成立；有几何有效的侧信息，并通过适当的超额风险估计选择超参数，则可实现实质性的一阶统计改进。该研究为理解和设计更优的数据驱动优化方法提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Recent proliferation of data-optimization integration has led to a range of methods that aim to improve the statistical performance of data-driven optimization decisions. However, while many of these methods are motivated intuitively from a robustness or regularization perspective, their resulting statistical benefits are often unclear and, even if available, are established on a case-by-case basis. We provide a systematic dissection of data-driven optimization formulations using the view of "directionally perturbed" empirical optimization (EO). Specifically, this umbrella of formulations, which we call "EO+", covers many existing data-driven optimization methods, including regularization, distributionally robust optimization, transfer learning, and analogous methods for contextual optimization. On the one hand, we argue that without additional, correctly specified, side information, any EO+ method can result in at most second-order improvements. This provides a negative conclusion, namely ``no free lunch is possible", on the statistical power of EO+. On the other hand, we show that when leveraging side information that is geometrically effective, achieving first-order improvements is possible by choosing hyperparameters that are significantly larger than what is typically suggested in the literature. Moreover, we construct a principled methodology based on excess risk estimation, via either system knowledge or bootstrap resampling, to maximize the first-order gain. We demonstrate how this gain connects to the control-variate principle, a variance reduction technique in the Monte Carlo simulation literature, which helps explain why geometrically effective side information is necessary.

</details>


### [120] [A Linearly Convergent Projection-Free Algorithm for Smooth Convex Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04321)
*Elad Hazan*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种投影自由的梯度下降方法，用于在光滑强凸目标函数上求解凸集约束优化问题。当可行集是光滑时，可以用一次梯度计算和一次支撑切平面计算替代每轮迭代中的投影操作，同时保持线性收敛速度。此外，所需的切平面可以通过约Õ(d)次成员查询近似到足够精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 投影梯度下降（PGD）虽然在线性收敛方面表现优异，但每轮迭代都需要计算投影到可行集的操作，这在可行集结构复杂时计算代价高昂。虽然已有一些投影自由方法（如Frank-Wolfe），但此前只有对多面体集合或同时光滑且强凸的集合才能保证线性收敛。本文旨在将投影自由线性收敛的适用范围扩展到所有光滑凸集。

Method: 提出了一种新的投影自由算法，核心思想是用支撑切平面（supporting tangent）替代投影。在每次迭代中，只需进行一次梯度计算和一次支撑切平面计算。对于光滑可行集，证明了该方案可以保持线性收敛。同时，证明了所需的支撑切平面可以通过O~(d)次成员查询（membership oracle）被逼近到足够精度，从而实现实用的实现。

Result: 在可行集为光滑的条件下，该方法实现了投影自由且线性的收敛速度。相比已有工作（仅适用于多面体集或同时光滑且强凸的集合），本文的结果推广到了任意光滑凸集（不需要强凸性）。支撑切平面的计算复杂度为O~(d)次成员查询。

Conclusion: 本文证明了在光滑凸可行集上，投影可以被支撑切平面计算所替代，同时保持投影梯度法的线性收敛速度。这一结果填补了此前投影自由线性收敛仅适用于多面体集或光滑且强凸集的空白，为复杂约束优化问题提供了更高效的计算途径。

Abstract: We consider minimizing a smooth, strongly convex function over a convex set. Projected gradient descent is known to converge linearly in this setting, but each iteration requires a projection onto the feasible set, which may be computationally expensive.
  We show that when the feasible set is smooth, projection can be replaced by one gradient computation and a single supporting-tangent computation per iteration, while preserving linear convergence. Moreover, the required tangent can be approximated to sufficient accuracy using $\widetilde O(d)$ membership-oracle queries, where $d$ is the ambient dimension. Previously, projection-free linear convergence was known only for polyhedral sets or for sets that are both smooth and strongly convex.

</details>


### [121] [An $α$-Potential Game Approach to $N$-Player Stochastic Linear-Quadratic Differential Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04386)
*Chenhui Hao,Jingtao Shi*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文从α-势博弈的角度研究N人随机线性二次(LQ)微分博弈。首先推导了带乘性噪声的闭环LQ博弈中代价函数线性导数的概率表示和PDE表示并证明等价性；随后构建了开环随机LQ α-势博弈框架，利用线性导数构造α势函数并给出近似参数α的上界，通过增广状态变量将势函数最小化转化为有限维随机控制问题，从而得到开环α-Nash均衡。最后应用于网络LQ博弈，证明所得反馈表示与现有条件McKean-Vlasov方法一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体LQ微分博弈在工程与经济领域有广泛应用，但求解N人博弈的Nash均衡通常十分困难。势博弈理论为这类问题提供了有力工具，因为势博弈的均衡可通过单一函数的优化来刻画。本文旨在将α-势博弈框架推广到带乘性噪声的N人随机LQ微分博弈，以简化均衡的求解过程。

Method: (1) 考虑带乘性噪声的闭环LQ博弈，其中漂移和扩散系数均线性依赖于状态和完整控制向量，推导代价函数一阶和二阶线性导数的概率表示与PDE表示并证明等价性；(2) 基于线性导数构造建立开环随机LQ α-势博弈框架，显式给出近似参数α关于模型系数和容许控制半径的上界；(3) 通过增广变分过程将α势函数的最小化转化为有限维随机控制问题，进而获得开环α-Nash均衡；(4) 将该方法应用于网络LQ博弈并与条件McKean-Vlasov方法进行比较验证。

Result: 成功建立了带乘性噪声的N人随机LQ微分博弈的α-势博弈理论框架，得到了线性导数的两种等价表示、α参数的显式上界，以及通过有限维随机控制问题求解开环α-Nash均衡的途径。在网络LQ博弈应用中，所得反馈表示与现有条件McKean-Vlasov方法的结果一致，但推导过程更直接。

Conclusion: 本文为N人随机LQ微分博弈提供了基于α-势博弈的新求解框架，将复杂的多智能体均衡问题化简为标准有限维LQ控制问题。该方法的优越性在于计算路径清晰且不依赖复杂的条件McKean-Vlasov技术，为大规模多智能体LQ博弈的高效求解提供了新思路。

Abstract: This paper studies $N$-player stochastic linear-quadratic (LQ) differential games from the perspective of $α$-potential games. We first consider a closed-loop LQ game with multiplicative noise, where both the drift and the diffusion coefficients depend linearly on the state and the full control vector. For this model, we derive probabilistic and partial differential equation (PDE) representations for the first- and second-order linear derivatives of the players' cost function and prove the equivalence between them. We then develop an open-loop stochastic LQ \(α\)-potential game framework. Using the linear derivative construction, we build an \(α\)-potential function and derive an explicit upper bound for the approximation parameter \(α\) in terms of the model coefficients and the admissible control radius. Moreover, the minimization of the \(α\)-potential function is reduced to a finite-dimensional stochastic control problem by augmenting the state with the variational process, which yields an open-loop \(α\)-Nash equilibrium. As an application, we revisit a network LQ game considered in \cite{GuoLiZhang2025} and show that the feedback representation obtained from our approach coincides with the feedback in the existing conditional McKean--Vlasov approach, while our characterization follows directly from a standard finite-dimensional LQ control problem.

</details>


### [122] [Incremental Aggregation on the Grassmannian for Asynchronous Eigenspace Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04406)
*Xiaolu Wang,Jiang Hu,Hoi-To Wai*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了面向异构分布式系统的Grassmann流形增量聚合方法，用于异步有限和特征空间计算，通过外在极坐标更新保持子空间几何，实现两阶段线性收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异步特征空间计算的理论基础稀缺，现有方法在陈旧信息下对Grassmann流形上的动力学覆盖有限，缺乏无需全局同步的低成本异步更新方案。

Method: 提出Grassmannian增量聚合方法：仅刷新到达的组件并复用缓存梯度，保持低单次更新成本且无需全局同步；采用外在极坐标更新以保持内在子空间几何，避免对陈旧切向量进行平行传输；建立了角度相关的梯度支配特性刻画和过时聚合更新的盆地不变性质。

Result: 分析得到两阶段线性收敛（显式宽盆地阶段和更尖锐的局部阶段），常数由分量谱扩展控制；在串行和分布式PCA实验中展现了更优的样本效率和墙钟收敛速度，优于代表性基线。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了异构分布式场景下异步流形优化理论空白，证明所提方法在理论和实验上均具显著优势，为大规模特征空间计算提供了高效异步方案。

Abstract: We study asynchronous optimization for finite-sum eigenspace computation in heterogeneous distributed systems. The theoretical foundations for asynchronous eigenspace computation remain scarce, with existing approaches offering limited coverage of dynamics directly on the Grassmannian under stale information. In this paper, we propose a Grassmannian incremental aggregation method that refreshes only arriving components and reuses cached gradients, retaining low per-update cost without global synchronization. The method employs an extrinsic polar update that preserves the intrinsic subspace geometry without requiring parallel transport of stale tangent vectors. Our analysis establishes a tight angle-dependent gradient-dominance characterization of the objective and a basin-invariance property for stale aggregated updates. These yield two-phase linear convergence, comprising an explicit broad-basin regime and a sharper local regime, with constants controlled by component spectral spreads. Experiments on serial and distributed PCA demonstrate improved sample efficiency and wall-clock convergence over representative baselines.

</details>


### [123] [A Counterexample to Fourier Alignment in Single-Neuron Modular Addition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04451)
*Gautam Neelakantan Memana*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文给出了MAIS-O60问题的否定解，构造了一个在模加法任务上训练单个ReLU神经元的反例：初始活跃的ReLU神经元在有限时间内变得完全失活，其傅里叶能量在所有非零实频类上均匀分布。该反例在初始条件的开集上成立，因此在高斯初始化下以正概率发生。附录进一步将反例推广到所有Clarke轨迹、光滑死区逼近ReLU以及固定步长全批量梯度下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 否定MAIS-O60猜想，即反驳"单频对齐"是模加法上训练单个神经元的一般性结果这一假设，展示在简单单神经元设置下也存在训练失败模式。

Method: 构造一个具体反例：ReLU神经元在模加法任务上训练，初始活跃但有限时间内ReLU输入变为负从而神经元完全失活，权重冻结在一个极限点，该极限的傅里叶能量在所有非零实频类上均匀分布。通过开集上的初始条件证明正概率发生，并以光滑死区逼近和固定步长全批量梯度下降扩展该结果。

Result: 成功构造了反例，显示存在开集的初始条件使得单ReLU神经元在有限时间内完全失活并冻结在傅里叶能量均匀分布的极限处。该反例可推广到所有Clarke轨迹（在ReLU'(0)=0约定下）、光滑死区逼近ReLU以及固定步长全批量梯度下降。

Conclusion: 单频对齐并非训练单神经元进行模加法的一般性结论，MAIS-O60猜想得到否定解答。即使在这个最简单的设置中，优化过程也可能因神经元完全失活而陷入傅里叶能量均匀分布的失败状态。

Abstract: We give a negative solution to MAIS-O60. We first construct an example in which an initially active ReLU neuron becomes completely inactive in finite time and thereafter remains frozen at a limit whose Fourier energy is equally distributed among all nonzero real frequency classes. The counterexample holds on an open set of initial conditions and therefore occurs with positive probability under Gaussian initialization. An appendix prepared by GPT-5.6 Sol strengthens the counterexample by showing that the same failure can occur for every Clarke trajectory from an open set of initial conditions, under the convention $\mathrm{ReLU}'(0)=0$, for smooth dead-zone approximations of ReLU, and for fixed-step full-batch gradient descent. Thus, single-frequency alignment is not a general consequence of training a single neuron on modular addition.

</details>


### [124] [Jointly Sparse Blind Deconvolution via Riemannian Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04465)
*Wenlong Wang,Baiyang Guo,Zai Yang,Shixiang Chen,Junpeng Shi*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用联合稀疏结构的盲反卷积优化方法，开发了黎曼优化算法，并建立了估计误差与样本复杂度之间的非渐近理论保证，证明联合稀疏性可显著降低成功恢复所需的样本复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 盲反卷积在系统辨识和信号处理中广泛应用，联合稀疏结构在实际场景中普遍存在，但如何有效利用该结构提升恢复性能仍然是一个具有挑战性且基本未解决的问题。

Method: 提出联合稀疏促进优化问题，并开发了黎曼优化算法来准确高效地求解该问题。

Result: 建立了估计误差与样本复杂度之间关系的非渐近理论保证，表明利用联合稀疏性可显著降低成功恢复所需的样本复杂度；数值实验验证了理论结果并展示了所提方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 利用联合稀疏结构能够显著提升盲反卷积的恢复性能并降低样本复杂度需求，所提出的黎曼优化算法在理论上和实验上均被证明有效。

Abstract: Blind deconvolution has been widely applied in system identification and signal processing. While joint sparsity commonly arises in practical scenarios, effectively exploiting this structure to enhance recovery performance remains a challenging and largely open problem. In this paper, we propose a joint-sparsity-promoting optimization problem and develop a Riemannian optimization algorithm for its accurate and efficient solution. We further establish theoretical guarantees that characterize the non-asymptotic relationship between the estimation error and the sample complexity, showing that exploiting joint sparsity can significantly reduce the sample complexity required for successful recovery. Numerical experiments are provided that validate the theoretical results and demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed approach.

</details>


### [125] [Variable Smoothing for Weakly Convex Problems with Non-Euclidean Directions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04584)
*Farid Najar*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出MELMO（Moreau包络光滑与线性最小化预言机）算法，用于求解f光滑、g可能非光滑的复合优化问题min_x f(x)+g(Tx)。该方法利用Moreau包络光滑非光滑分量，并通过线性最小化预言机适配问题几何结构。在g为ρ-弱凸的假设下，建立了由步长与光滑调度参数化的收敛界族，明确刻画了优化光滑目标与恢复原始复合问题驻点之间的权衡。特别地，一个机制对光滑梯度范数和复合驻点代理均达到O(k^{-1/4})速率，另一机制对光滑梯度范数达O(k^{-1/3})、对复合代理达O(k^{-1/4})，此外还建立了依赖K-水平线的收敛率（复合代理达O(K^{-1/3})）。实验表明MELMO在稀疏低秩矩阵分解和图像去噪上与变分光滑和次梯度基线相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复合优化问题是机器学习和信号处理中的核心问题，其中目标函数包含光滑部分与非光滑部分（如正则项）。现有方法（如次梯度法或近端法）在处理非光滑项时要么收敛速度慢，要么对问题结构（如线性算子T的几何结构）缺乏适应性。作者希望通过结合Moreau包络的光滑化特性与线性最小化预言机（LMO）的几何适配能力，设计一种既能获得更好收敛速率又能适应广泛问题结构的统一算法框架，并显式刻画平滑化与该化之间的权衡。

Method: MELMO算法通过Moreau包络对非光滑分量g进行光滑化，将原复合问题转化为更易求解的光滑问题，同时利用线性最小化预言机（LMO）而非投影操作来适配问题几何（如稀疏低秩等结构）。算法在g为ρ-弱凸的假设下，通过步长与光滑参数的调度策略，实现了对光滑目标优化与原始问题驻点恢复之间权衡的显式控制。

Result: 理论方面：建立了参数化收敛界族，其中一种机制对光滑梯度范数与复合驻点代理均获得O(k^{-1/4})速率；另一种机制对光滑梯度范数获得O(k^{-1/3})、对复合代理获得O(k^{-1/4})速率；还推导了依赖K-水平线的收敛率，复合代理可达O(K^{-1/3})。实验方面：在稀疏低秩矩阵分解和图像去噪任务上，MELMO与变分光滑和次梯度基线方法性能相当。

Conclusion: MELMO通过Moreau包络光滑化与LMO的结合，为包含非光滑复合项的优化问题提供了统一的收敛性分析框架。算法在理论上明确了不同类型收敛速率之间的权衡关系，在实验上展现了实用性。该工作为弱凸非光滑复合优化的算法设计提供了新的思路，其速率参数化的框架也便于后续针对具体问题选择最优调度策略。

Abstract: We propose MELMO (Moreau Envelope Smoothing with Linear Minimization Oracles), an algorithm for composite optimization problems of the form min x f (x) + g(T x), where f is smooth and g may be non-smooth. The method leverages the Moreau envelope to smooth the non-smooth component while adapting to problem geometry through linear minimization oracles. Assuming g is $ρ$-weakly convex, we establish a family of convergence bounds parameterized by the step-size and smoothing schedules, thereby making explicit the trade-off between optimizing the smoothed objective and recovering stationarity for the original composite problem. In particular, one regime yields O(k -1/4 ) rates for both the smoothed-gradient norm and a composite stationarity proxy, while another yields O(k -1/3 ) for the smoothed-gradient norm together with O(k -1/4 ) for the composite proxy. We also establish a K-horizon-dependent convergence rate that yields O(K -1/3 ) for the composite proxy. Empirically, MELMO is competitive with variable smoothing and subgradient baselines on sparse low-rank matrix factorization and image denoising.

</details>


### [126] [On MUON optimization: From non-convergence to an error analysis with Polar Express and the Newton-Schulz polynomial from implementations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04607)
*Thang Do,Steffen Dereich,Arnulf Jentzen*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出并研究了一种广义的MUON优化器变体，支持任意数量的广义Newton-Schulz矩阵步骤（可包含任意高次多项式），涵盖原始MUON和MUON+Polar Express两种特例。对于一类简单随机优化问题，证明了几乎对所有mini-batch大小MUON都无法收敛到解；同时建立了广义NS步骤下MUON的误差分析，给出梯度步骤数和mini-batch大小的收敛率，并在二次随机优化问题和l2正则化逻辑回归中进行了实例验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MUON优化器是2024年提出的加速SGD变体，通过利用DNN权重参数的矩阵结构并采用五个Newton-Schulz步骤来提升训练效率，在LLM等AI系统中被广泛使用。然而，目前缺乏对MUON变体的系统理论研究，尤其是其收敛性和误差分析尚不充分。本文旨在推广MUON框架并提供严格的数学分析。

Method: 本文提出广义MUON优化器框架，允许使用任意数量的广义Newton-Schulz步骤（可采用任意高次多项式）。该框架将原始MUON（使用原始NS多项式）和MUON+Polar Express方法作为特例。作者对一类简单随机优化问题进行分析，研究几乎任意mini-batch大小下MUON的收敛行为，并建立误差分析以推导关于梯度步骤数和mini-batch大小的收敛率。

Result: 主要结果包括：(1) 对于简单类别的随机优化问题，证明了在几乎所有的mini-batch大小下，随着梯度步骤数趋于无穷，MUON无法收敛到SOP的解；(2) 建立了广义NS步骤下MUON的误差分析，提供了以梯度步骤数和mini-batch大小为变量的收敛率；(3) 在二次随机优化问题和l2正则化逻辑回归（处理二分类）等具体实例中验证了误差分析结果。

Conclusion: 本文为广义MUON优化器提供了严格的理论分析框架，揭示了其在某些随机优化场景下的收敛性限制（不收敛性），同时给出了可量化的误差界与收敛率，为MUON类方法在实际AI系统中的应用提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Stochastic gradient descent (SGD) optimization methods are the standard instruments for the training of deep neural networks (DNNs). In many relevant artificial intelligence (AI) systems - such as popular large language models (LLMs)-not the standard SGD scheme is used as the optimization method but instead suitable accelerated variants of SGD are employed. One of the most popular methods of such accelerated SGD variants is the momentum orthogonalized by Newton-Schulz (MUON) optimizer proposed by Jordan et al. in 2024. The MUON optimizer exploits the special matrix structure of the weight parameters in the training of the DNNs and, in its original form, employs five Newton-Schultz (NS) matrix steps in each MUON iteration.
  In this work we propose and study a generalized variant of the MUON optimizer involving an arbitrary number of generalized NS steps with polynomials of possibly arbitrary high degree. The considered optimizer covers MUON with the original NS polynomial as well as MUON combined with the recently proposed Polar Express method as special cases. For a simple class of stochastic optimization problems (SOPs) we show for almost every mini-batch size that MUON fails to converge to the solution of the SOP as the number of gradient steps converges to infinity. We also establish an error analysis for MUON with the generalized NS steps that provides convergence rates in terms of the number of gradient steps and in terms of the size of the mini-batch. We illustrate our general error analysis for MUON in the case of several concrete examples including quadratic stochastic optimization problems (SOPs) as well as $\ell_2$ regularized logistic regression for binary classification.

</details>


### [127] [Primal-dual multigrid methods for nonsmooth optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04848)
*Felipe Guerra,Tuomo Valkonen*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将原始-对偶算法与多重网格技术相结合的方法，用于求解形如 min_x F(x)+E(x)+G(Kx) 的优化问题。通过引入非光滑原始-对偶一致性条件和高效的部分线性化线搜索过程来连接粗细网格问题，并在全变差正则化逆成像问题上验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多逆成像等优化问题具有复合结构，传统的前向-后向多重网格方法在求解这类问题时存在局限，无法处理某些类型的非光滑项或耦合算子。作者希望将原始-对偶算法与多重网格相结合，以解决此前前向-后向多重网格方法无法解决的问题。

Method: 将原始-对偶算法与多重网格技术相结合；引入一种非光滑原始-对偶一致性条件，用于在粗细网格之间建立耦合关系；设计了高效的部分线性化线搜索过程以加速收敛。

Result: 在全变差正则化逆成像问题上验证了方法的有效性，能够解决此前前向-后向多重网格方法无法求解的问题。

Conclusion: 该方法的创新在于打通了原始-对偶框架与多重网格加速之间的桥梁，通过非光滑一致性条件和部分线性化线搜索，显著扩展了多重网格方法在复合优化问题上的适用范围，尤其在全变差正则化逆成像领域展现出色性能。

Abstract: In optimization, one often encounters problems of the form $\min_x F(x)+E(x)+G(Kx)$. In this work, we combine primal-dual algorithms with multigrid techniques for their solution. To link the the fine-grid and coarse-grid problems problems, we introduce a nonsmooth primal-dual coherence condition, and an efficient partially linearized line search procedure. Our work is motivated by total variation regularized inverse imaging problems, on which we demonstrate the efficacy of the method, being able to solve problems not previously possible with forward-backward multigrid methods.

</details>


### [128] [A Few Accelerated Algorithms for Convex Optimization under $(H_0,H_1)$-Smoothness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04884)
*Aleksandr Lobanov*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对凸(H_0,H_1)-光滑优化问题提出了加速的全梯度和随机坐标方法，分别通过Nesterov型加速梯度结合小维松弛与相位重启，以及均匀/非均匀采样策略，获得了首个针对该凸类的加速理论保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准光滑度假设不足以刻画许多实际问题中Hessian范数以目标函数残差为界线增长的结构。已有(L_0,L_1)-光滑类的研究主要针对非凸情形，而对凸情形加速方法的研究仍缺失，亟需在此类更一般的凸光滑设定下建立加速算法及其复杂度保证。

Method: 将Nesterov型加速梯度方案与小维松弛（small-dimensional relaxation）和相位重启（phase restarts）相结合，构造全梯度方法；并将相同框架推广到随机坐标优化，分别设计了均匀采样（迭代复杂度含标准因子d）和非均匀采样（迭代复杂度由S_{1/2}^{(j)}刻画）的坐标方法。

Result: 全梯度方法迭代复杂度为Õ(√(H_0 R̃²/ε)+√(H_1 R̃²)log(F_0/ε))；均匀采样坐标方法复杂度含因子d，非均匀采样坐标方法复杂度由S_{1/2}^{(j)}控制。实验验证了加速度效果、非均匀采样的收益以及非精确松弛的可行性。

Conclusion: 该工作首次为该凸(H_0,H_1)-光滑类提供了加速全梯度与坐标优化保证，并给出了实用的实现建议，实验证明了理论加速的有效性。

Abstract: We develop accelerated algorithms for convex $(H_0,H_1)$-smooth optimization, where $\|\nabla^2 f(x)\|\le H_0+H_1(f(x)-f^*)$. This class generalizes standard smoothness and contains the $(L_0,L_1)$-smooth class. Combining a Nesterov-type accelerated gradient scheme with small-dimensional relaxation and phase restarts, we obtain a full-gradient method with iteration complexity $\widetilde O(\sqrt{H_0\widetilde R^2/\varepsilon}+\sqrt{H_1\widetilde R^2}\log(F_0/\varepsilon))$. We extend the same approach to randomized coordinate optimization, obtaining a coordinate method with uniform sampling whose iteration complexity carries the standard factor $d$, and a coordinate method with non-uniform sampling whose iteration complexity is governed by $S_{1/2}^{(j)}=\sum_i\sqrt{H_{j,i}}$. These results provide, to our knowledge, the first accelerated full-gradient and coordinate guarantees for this convex class. We also provide practical implementation recommendations. Experiments confirm the predicted acceleration, gains from non-uniform sampling, and the viability of inexact relaxation.

</details>


### [129] [A Two-Stage Operating Room Allocation Framework for Reducing Surgical Waiting Lists in Public Hospitals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04978)
*Jhoan Báez,Juan Hoyos,Víctor Riquelme,Michel Royer,Héctor Ramírez*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个两阶段手术室分配框架，用于专科排程环境，结合混合整数线性规划与基于优先级的患者分配程序，在模拟实验中相比集成基线方法实现了更均衡的手术时间分配、更高的手术室利用率及大幅降低的计算时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公立医院面临手术能力有限和大量择期手术等待名单的挑战，手术室分配是重大难题，需要一种既高效又实用的分配策略来减少手术等待名单。

Method: 提出两阶段手术室分配框架：第一阶段使用混合整数线性规划模型进行专科病区分配；第二阶段采用基于优先级的患者分配程序。与文献中的集成混合整数线性规划基线方法进行比较，并在包括患者确认失败和手术暂停等现实运营干扰条件下，通过一周和多周模拟场景进行评估。

Result: 该框架在不同专科间实现了手术提供时间与需求时间之间更均衡的分配，同时保持了较高的手术室利用率，计算时间显著低于集成基线方法。在20周场景中，该方法的等待时间更短，手术患者数量更大，同时保持运营灵活性。

Conclusion: 基于分解的手术室分配策略为公立医院减少手术等待名单提供了一种实用且计算高效的替代方案。

Abstract: Operating-room allocation is a major challenge for public hospitals with limited surgical capacity and large elective waiting lists. This work proposes a two-stage operating-room allocation framework for specialty-block scheduling environments. The methodology combines a mixed-integer linear programming model for medical specialty block allocation with a priority-based patient allocation procedure. The framework was evaluated through one-week and multi-week simulation scenarios using parameters estimated from historical surgical and waiting-list data. The proposed methodology was compared against an integrated mixed-integer linear programming baseline adapted from the literature under realistic operational disruptions, including failed patient confirmations and surgery suspensions. Results show that the proposed framework achieves a more balanced distribution between offered and demanded surgical time across specialties while maintaining high operating-room utilization and substantially lower computational times than the integrated baseline. In the 20-week scenario, the proposed methodology achieved lower waiting times and operated a larger number of patients while preserving operational flexibility. These results suggest that decomposition-based operating-room allocation strategies provide a practical and computationally efficient alternative for reducing surgical waiting lists in public hospitals.

</details>


### [130] [On the Iterate Convergence of Bregman Projected Gradient Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05035)
*He Chen,Anthony Man-Cho So*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对Bregman投影梯度法（BPGM）在Shannon熵核下的迭代收敛这一长期开放问题，提出了一个全新的收敛分析框架，核心是提出"尺度Kurdyka-Łojasiewicz（SKŁ）"性质，该性质保证了BPGM的迭代收敛性，并适用于所有连续次解析函数；同时证明了SKŁ指数为1/2时序列线性收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BPGM的迭代收敛问题长期以来悬而未决，特别是对于被广泛采用的Shannon熵核。现有的收敛结果往往受限于核的梯度Lipschitz连续性或目标函数的严格条件，严重限制了该方法的理论保证和实际应用范围。

Method: 提出一个新的"尺度Kurdyka-Łojasiewicz（SKŁ）"性质来描述函数在Bregman几何下的局部增长行为，基于此建立BPGM在Shannon熵核下的迭代收敛分析框架。进一步证明：1) SKŁ性质保证BPGM的迭代收敛且对所有连续次解析函数成立；2) 当问题满足SKŁ指数为1/2时，BPGM序列线性收敛；3) SKŁ指数1/2可由严格互补性和目标梯度局部Lipschitz连续性条件下的KŁ指数1/2推出。

Result: 在新框架下获得了BPGM在Shannon熵核下对广泛一类线性约束目标函数的强收敛结果；证明了SKŁ性质对连续次解析函数的普适性；建立了SKŁ指数1/2与线性收敛之间的等价关系，并给出了SKŁ指数1/2成立的具体充要条件。

Conclusion: 该工作首次系统地解决了BPGM在Shannon熵核下迭代收敛的开放问题，为Bregman投影梯度法的理论分析开辟了新路径，并为后续在更广泛问题中应用BPGM提供了坚实的理论支撑。

Abstract: The iterate convergence of \textit{Bregman projected gradient method} (BPGM) has remained a long-standing open problem, especially for the widely adopted Shannon entropy kernel. Existing convergence results are often limited, relying on Lipschitz continuity of the kernel's gradient or restrictive conditions on the objective function. In this paper, we develop a novel convergence analysis framework for the BPGM with the Shannon entropy kernel, yielding strong convergence results for a broad class of objective functions under linear constraints. The cornerstone of our framework is a new concept called \textit{scaled Kurdyka-Łojasiewicz} (SKŁ) property, which captures the local growth behavior of a function under the Bregman geometry. We show that the SKŁ property ensures the iterate convergence of BPGM and holds for all continuous subanalytical functions. Furthermore, we prove that the BPGM sequence exhibits linear convergence if the problem possesses an SKŁ exponent of $1/2$. We then furnish the examples of functions with the SKŁ exponent $1/2$ by proving that the SKŁ exponent $1/2$ is implied by the KŁ exponent $1/2$ under strict complementarity and local Lipschitz continuity of the objective's gradient. Building on these novel results, our work takes a first step towards resolving the open problem of BPGM iterate convergence.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [131] [C$^2$MOE: Consistency and Complementarity-guided Mixture of Experts for Incomplete Multimodal Emotion Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04013)
*Yuntao Shou,Tao Meng,Wei Ai,Keqin Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出C2MOE框架，通过将多模态知识分解为一致性和互补性两部分，并在信息论框架下统一表示学习与缺失模态插补，有效解决了多模态情感识别中模态缺失导致的性能退化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态情感识别（MERC）依赖完整的模态输入，但实际场景中因传输错误或用户行为常导致模态缺失，严重降低模型性能。现有方法通过跨模态一致性学习增强鲁棒性，但忽略了模态间的互补性，导致重建结果存在偏差。

Method: C2MOE框架基于信息论原理，将多模态知识分解为一致性（通过最大化跨模态可预测性捕获）和互补性（通过最大化条件熵保留）两个部分。采用交互感知专家进行分解，并引入双分支预测机制：一致性分支通过最小化不确定性对齐插补特征与联合分布，互补性分支通过熵最大化利用模态独有线索。最后通过可学习的重加权模块动态分配各专家输出的重要性，实现鲁棒自适应的融合插补。

Result: 在多个MERC基准数据集上的大量实验表明，C2MOE在各种模态缺失设置下始终优于最先进的方法，验证了其鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: C2MOE通过同时建模模态一致性与互补性，并在信息论框架下统一表示学习和缺失模态插补，有效解决现有方法忽略模态互补性导致偏差重建的问题，为不完备多模态情感识别提供了更鲁棒的解决方案。

Abstract: Recent advances in Multimodal Emotion Recognition in Conversations (MERC) highlight its reliance on complete multimodal inputs. However, real-world data often suffer from missing modalities due to transmission errors or user behavior, severely degrading model performance. Existing methods enhance robustness via cross-modal consistency learning but largely ignore modality complementarity, leading to biased reconstructions. To address this limitation, we propose C$2$MOE, a novel Consistency and Complementarity-guided Mixture of Experts framework for incomplete multimodal emotion learning. Our approach unifies representation learning and missing modality imputation within a principled information-theoretic framework. Specifically, multimodal knowledge is factorized into consistency and complementarity components via interaction-aware experts. Consistency is captured by maximizing cross-modal predictability, while complementarity is preserved by maximizing conditional entropy between modalities. Building upon this decomposition, C$2$MOE introduces a dual-branch prediction mechanism for robust imputation under missing modalities. The consistency branch aligns imputed features with the joint distribution by minimizing uncertainty, and the complementarity branch exploits modality-unique cues via entropy maximization. Finally, C$2$MOE employs a learnable reweighting module that dynamically assigns importance scores to each expert's output, yielding a robust and adaptive fusion for imputation. Extensive experiments on multiple MERC benchmarks demonstrate that C$2$MOE consistently surpasses state-of-the-art methods across various missing-modality settings, validating its robustness and generalization.

</details>


### [132] [On Hamming-Lipschitz Type Stability of the Subdominant (Minmax) Ultrametric: Theory and Simple Proofs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04014)
*Alokendu Mazumder,Arnab Roy,Punit Rathore*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对最小上超度量（subdominant/minmax ultrametric）这一由单链聚类诱导的树结构摘要算子，开发了一套ℓ₀型的稳定性理论。研究发现稀疏编辑只通过最小生成树传播：超度量值仅在树路径穿越被编辑边或编辑的树外边暴露的切割时才会变化。文章给出了每个编辑的暴露切割分数、仅依赖树的全局包络、Hamming-Lipschitz界限以及尖锐性结果，并证明了多编辑条件下的近可加性原理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的超度量稳定性理论通常基于ℓ∞或Gromov-Hausdorff度量，但这些界限不适用于只改变少数成对距离的稀疏扰动场景。实际应用中（如深度嵌入图）的层级表示往往面临稀疏编辑，需要一种针对稀疏扰动的稳定性框架来理解和诊断层级结构表示的脆弱性。

Method: 作者建立了超度量运算符的ℓ₀型稳定性理论。核心方法包括：(1) 分析稀疏编辑如何通过最小生成树传播——只有树路径穿越被编辑边或编辑的树外边暴露的切割时，成对超度量值才会改变；(2) 定义每个编辑的暴露切割分数和仅依赖树几何的全局包络；(3) 推导Hamming-Lipschitz界限来约束可改变的超度量条目数量；(4) 证明尖锐性结果：严格切割分离下树边界限精确达到，树外编辑存在改变Θ(n²)个超度量条目的显式族；(5) 证明多编辑条件下的条件近可加性原理。

Result: 研究结果表明：稀疏编辑对超度量值的影响严格受限于MST结构，每个编辑的传播范围由暴露切割分数刻画。证实了对树几何的依赖不可规避——树边编辑在严格切割分离下精确达到界限，树外编辑可导致一个编辑改变Θ(n²)个条目。在深度嵌入图上的实验验证了所得结构分数作为层级表示脆弱性诊断工具的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文为最小上超度量提供了ℓ₀型稳定性理论，填补了传统ℓ∞/Gromov-Hausdorff稳定性理论在稀疏扰动场景下的空白。该理论不仅给出了尖锐的传播界限和可加性原理，还为层级结构表示的脆弱性分析提供了可操作的结构分数。

Abstract: The subdominant (minmax) ultrametric is a canonical tree-structured summary of a dissimilarity matrix, arising equivalently as the ultrametric induced by single-linkage clustering. While its classical stability theory is usually formulated in $\ell_\infty$ or Gromov--Hausdorff terms, such bounds are poorly suited to sparse perturbations that alter only a few pairwise distances. We develop an $\ell_0$-type stability theory for this operator. Our analysis shows that sparse edits propagate only through the minimum spanning tree (MST): a pairwise ultrametric value can change only if its tree path crosses an edited edge or a cut newly exposed by an edited off-tree edge. This yields a sharp per-edit exposed-cut score and a tree-only global envelope, leading to Hamming--Lipschitz bounds on the number of ultrametric entries that can change. We also prove sharpness results showing that this dependence on tree geometry is unavoidable: under strict cut separation the tree-edge bound is attained exactly, and for off-tree edits there are explicit families in which one edited distance changes $Θ(n^2)$ ultrametric entries. In addition, we prove a conditional near-additivity principle for multiple edits under certified large per-edit changed regions and negligible aggregate overlap. Experiments on deep-embedding graphs show that the resulting structural scores provide useful vulnerability diagnostics for hierarchical representations.

</details>


### [133] [A Trust-region Framework for Moment Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04026)
*Oluwasegun A. Somefun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个信赖域框架来统一理解Adam等自适应矩估计优化机制，推导出一族基于二阶矩估计和归一化p阶矩估计的学习率机制（Gmake）。当p=4时涉及峰度估计。实验表明，在弱信赖域约束下四阶矩版本更有优势，而在强约束下二阶矩版本更具竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Adam等自适应矩估计优化器在实践中表现优异，但其行为缺乏统一的理论解释框架。作者希望建立一个统一的信赖域框架，从原理层面解释和推导这类优化机制的运作方式，并探索更高阶矩估计（如四阶矩/峰度）带来的潜在收益。

Method: 作者提出了一个信赖域框架，其中每个权重的更新步长大小被限制在由p阶（p∈[2,4]）矩约束所控制的信赖区域内。基于此推导出一族学习率机制Gmake，其核心是基于二阶矩估计和归一化p阶矩估计。当p=4时，涉及峰度类估计。该框架统一了矩归一化、学习率调度、作为动量的谱低通滤波以及算子级谱归一化。

Result: 在GPT2-124M模型上使用FineWeb-Edu和TinyStories数据集进行实验。结果表明：当信赖域约束较弱时，四阶矩（p=4）实现在验证损失上表现最佳；随着引入逐渐增强的信赖域控制，二阶矩（p=2）实现变得更具竞争力，通常能达到略低于对应四阶矩实现的验证损失。

Conclusion: 信赖域视角为理解自适应矩估计提供了统一框架；四阶矩估计的优势依赖于信赖域约束的强度，在弱约束下更有效，而在强约束下传统二阶矩估计（Adam类方法）更具优势。这为选择不同阶矩的优化机制提供了条件性指导。

Abstract: In this paper, we develop a trust-region framework for understanding the behavior of adaptive moment estimation mechanisms, such as \textsc{Adam}, in stochastic gradient optimization. Specifically, in this framework, the magnitude of the update step for each individual weight is constrained within a trust-region governed by a moment constraint of order $p\in[2,4]$. The resulting derivation then leads to a family of learning-rate mechanisms based on second-moment estimation and a normalized $p$-th moment estimation. When $p=4$, this involves kurtosis-like estimation. The general mechanism, referred to as \textsc{Gmake}, provides a unified interpretation of normalization by moment estimation, learning-rate scheduling, spectral lowpass filtering as momentum, and operator-level spectral normalization within a common trust-region framework. Experiments on GPT2-124M trained on FineWeb-Edu and TinyStories suggest that the fourth-moment realization provides its greatest benefit when trust-region constraints are weak. As progressively stronger trust-region controls are introduced, the second-moment realization becomes increasingly competitive, often achieving slightly lower validation loss than its corresponding fourth-moment realization.

</details>


### [134] [Learning to Resolve Neutron Resonances with Fully Convolutional Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04027)
*Nataly R. Panczyk,Athanasios Stamatopoulos,Josef Svoboda,Majdi I. Radaideh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探讨将机器学习框架与传统R-Matrix代码结合，用于自动检测中子透射谱中的共振峰。初步研究表明，虽然全卷积神经网络在七条透射谱上达到93%的分类准确率，但该指标夸大了模型的泛化能力——模型无法可靠地推广到未见过的同位素，未来需要更大的训练数据集并结合物理特征来改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统R-Matrix代码在拟合中子透射数据时依赖先验评估且需要大量人工干预，而透射数据本身复杂且充满噪声，传统峰值识别方法难以直接应用。研究旨在加速后实验数据处理流程并减少对先验评估的依赖所带来的偏差。

Method: 采用全卷积神经网络（FCN）对透射谱中每个点进行分类，区分其属于共振区或非共振区。模型在七条透射谱上进行训练和评估，其中包含两条评估数据和五条实验数据；并在此前PHYSOR 2026工作的基础上增加了额外训练数据。

Result: 模型在分类准确率上达到约93%，但进一步分析表明该准确率指标高估了模型的泛化能力。即使引入了更多训练数据，模型仍无法在未见的同位素上可靠泛化。

Conclusion: 当前方法尚不能可靠地泛化到未知同位素。未来工作应评估更大、更多样化的训练数据集能否提升泛化性能，同时需要将中子共振已知的物理特征整合到模型中以提高性能。

Abstract: This work investigates the feasibility of augmenting traditional R-Matrix codes with a robust machine learning framework for automatically detecting neutron resonances in transmission spectra. Neutron transmission data are often complex and noisy, making them difficult to analyze using traditional peak-identification methods. The state-of-the-art R-Matrix codes currently used by physicists to fit these data often depend on prior evaluations and require substantial manual effort. This preliminary study demonstrates a method for accelerating the post-experimental processing of neutron transmission data and reducing bias associated with dependence on prior evaluations. We employ a fully convolutional neural network to classify individual points as belonging to resonance or non-resonance regions in seven transmission spectra---two evaluated and five experimental. Although the model achieves classification accuracies in the range of 93\%, further analysis shows that this metric overstates its ability to generalize. Building on our prior analysis in PHYSOR 2026, we find that, despite the inclusion of additional training data, the method does not generalize reliably to previously unseen isotopes. To address these limitations, future work should evaluate whether a larger and more diverse training dataset can produce a generalizable model and should incorporate known physical characteristics of neutron resonances to improve model performance.

</details>


### [135] [Lindblad-Inspired Multi-Timescale Reservoir Computing with Separable Rotation and Dissipation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04028)
*Jyotiranjan Beuria,Amit Shukla*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种受Lindblad开放系统动力学启发的多时间尺度回声状态网络（multi-timescale reservoir），将循环算子分解为精确离散化的阻尼旋转模态，使旋转（相位混合）与衰减（记忆遗忘）成为独立的设计变量，并直接提供全局稳定性保证，在多个基准上取得优异或具有竞争力的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统回声状态网络的储层由一个随机循环矩阵同时承担信号混合、记忆保留和稳定性控制，难以对可逆混合与不可逆遗忘进行独立调控，也缺乏直接的全局稳定性保证。现有结构化设计虽改进了拓扑、范数保持或深度，但未能将旋转与衰减作为分离的模态控制变量。

Method: 提出一种基于Lindblad开放系统动力学的多时间尺度储层：循环算子由精确离散化的阻尼旋转模态组装而成。旋转与衰减作为独立设计变量分别控制相位混合与记忆损失；正交模态混合保持算子的正规性（normality），衰减谱直接决定回声状态稳定性裕度，无需事后调整谱半径。方法评估涵盖十个对齐种子，对比标准、泄漏、深度、正交、循环及新一代储层，以及一个紧凑训练的GRU。

Result: 在有界NARMA-20任务上取得最佳固定储层性能，在Lorenz-63上达到最低平均误差，在线性记忆任务上与最强结果持平，并在其他基准上广泛保持竞争力。消融实验表明旋转增加状态多样性，耗散提供受控遗忘并改善预测条件数。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了一种可解释的循环架构，其中混合（mixing）、记忆（memory）与稳定性（stability）是显式且可独立调节的设计变量，实现了开放系统动力学原理与结构化状态空间建模的有效融合。

Abstract: Echo-state networks enable efficient temporal learning by fixing the recurrent dynamics and training only a linear readout. However, conventional reservoirs typically accommodate signal mixing, memory retention, and stability within a single random recurrent matrix. Existing structured designs improve topology, norm preservation, leakage, or depth, but generally do not provide separate modal control of reversible mixing and irreversible forgetting together with a direct global stability guarantee. We introduce a classical Lindblad-inspired multi-timescale reservoir that bridges open-system dynamical principles with structured state-space modeling. The recurrent operator is assembled from exactly discretized damped rotational modes, so rotation and decay become independent design variables governing phase mixing and memory loss. Orthogonal mode mixing preserves normality, while the decay spectrum directly determines the echo-state stability margin without post-hoc spectral-radius rescaling. We evaluate the method over ten aligned seeds against standard, leaky, deep, orthogonal, cycle, and next-generation reservoirs, together with a compact trained gated recurrent unit, across linear memory, nonlinear recurrence, chaotic forecasting, delayed logic, and real sensor calibration. Across the benchmark suite, the proposed reservoir achieves the best fixed-reservoir performance on bounded NARMA-20 and the lowest mean error on Lorenz-63, matches the strongest linear-memory result, and remains broadly competitive across broad range of benchmarks. Ablation studies show that rotation increases state diversity, whereas dissipation provides controlled forgetting and improves predictive conditioning. The resulting framework offers an interpretable recurrent architecture in which mixing, memory, and stability are explicit and independently tunable design variables.

</details>


### [136] [An Explainable LLM Agent Layer for Open-World Anomaly Detection in Oil Wells](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04041)
*Lucas Gouveia Omena Lopes,Thales Miranda de Almeida Vieira,Eduardo Toledo de Lima Junior,William Wagner Matos Lira*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出在Open-World Learning (OWL)石油井异常检测管道下游放置一个大型语言模型(LLM)代理层，作为上游已发表方法的"伴侣"而非替代品。该代理使用Qwen3.5-397B-A17B MoE模型（经NVIDIA NIM服务），接收结构化传感器指标和上游分类/新颖性断言，返回自然语言论证、置信度排序的批评性反馈和统一的新颖性命名。在3W数据集的989个真实油井文件段上，代理在9类分类中达到35.1% top-1 / 63.9% top-3，在7个探测类上达到71.7% top-2验证（精确率0.91），以及89.7%的新颖性检测率且5/7隐藏类命名稳定。其核心角色是确认上游决策、以传感器可审计语言论证决策、标记不合理标签争议、并为聚类新颖性命名，旨在缩小阻碍OWL管道实际部署的可解释性差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有OWL管道（结合自编码器检测、多类分类和Mahalanobis新颖性检测）能回答"发生了什么"，但无法解释"模型为何这样认为"或"操作员下一步该做什么"，且无法为发现的新颖性集群赋予人类可读名称。这种可解释性差距阻碍了OWL管道在实际运营环境中的部署。

Method: 在已有OWL管道下游添加一个LLM代理层。代理使用Qwen3.5-397B-A17B Mixture-of-Experts模型，通过NVIDIA NIM服务部署。代理接收来自上游的传感器结构指标和分类/新颖性断言，并输出：(1)自然语言决策论证；(2)置信度排序的批评性反馈；(3)检测到新颖性的统一命名。代理的作用包括确认合理的上游决策、论证决策、标记不合理标签的争议，以及为未标记事件提供统一的类人可读标签。

Result: 在3W数据集989个真实油井文件段上，三个研究结果表明：9类全分类top-1准确率35.1%，top-3准确率63.9%（95% CI [56.9, 70.4]）；7个探测类top-2验证准确率71.7% [64.8, 77.6]，精确率0.91 [0.84, 0.95]；新颖性检测率89.7% [87.0, 91.9]，且7个隐藏类中有5个实现了稳定的集群命名。

Conclusion: LLM代理不是独立的分类器，而是作为OWL管道的可解释性伴侣，用于确认上游决策、以可审计的传感器语言论证决策、标记不合理分歧并为新颖性命名。该方法有效缩小了OWL管道从研究到实际运营部署之间的可解释性差距。

Abstract: Open-World Learning (OWL) pipelines for oil well anomaly detection have recently been shown to combine autoencoder-based detection, multiclass classification, and Mahalanobis-based novelty detection on the public 3W dataset. These pipelines answer \textit{what happened}, but they do not explain \textit{why the model believes it} or \textit{what the operator should do next}, and they do not put a human-readable name on the novelty clusters they discover. This paper evaluates a Large Language Model (LLM) agent layer placed downstream of the OWL pipeline, designed as a \textbf{companion} to the published upstream methods rather than a replacement. Using the Qwen3.5-397B-A17B Mixture-of-Experts model served via NVIDIA NIM, the agent receives structured sensor metrics and upstream classification or novelty assertions, and returns natural-language justifications, confidence-ranked critiques, and consolidated names for detected novelties. Across three studies spanning 989 real well-file segments from the 3W dataset, the agent achieved $35.1\%$ top-1 / $63.9\%$ top-3 (95\% CI [56.9, 70.4]) classification on all nine classes, $71.7\%$ top-2 validation [64.8, 77.6] with precision $0.91$ [0.84, 0.95] across 7 probed classes, and $89.7\%$ novelty detection [87.0, 91.9] with stable cluster naming on 5 of 7 hidden classes. The agent is not a standalone classifier. Its role is to: (1) confirm upstream decisions when sensor evidence supports them, (2) justify decisions in sensor-grounded language operators can audit, (3) flag disagreement when upstream labels are implausible, and (4) name novelties so that clustered unlabeled events arrive at the engineer with a consolidated human-readable label. The goal is to close the explainability gap that currently blocks deployment of OWL pipelines in operational settings.

</details>


### [137] [Tactus: Open-Vocabulary Object Recognition from Low-Cost Pressure Arrays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04043)
*Abdul Basit Tonmoy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Tactus是一个基于电阻式压力阵列的开放性触觉模型，仅依靠压力数据即可回答文本查询。在STAG基准上达到0.771±0.062的top-1准确率，匹配甚至超越监督式闭集CNN（0.76），且无需训练分类头。该模型采用小数据配方：187条训练记录、14.4万帧未标记同传感器数据的掩码自编码器预训练，以及传感器自身的校准仿射变换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电阻式压力阵列是最便宜、出货量最大的触觉传感器，但现有的触觉表征学习研究几乎全部聚焦于光学传感器（对变形凝胶成像）。本文旨在填补这一空白，证明仅凭压力数据也能实现强大的触觉表征与文本查询能力。

Method: 使用掩码自编码器（MAE）在144k未标记的同传感器帧上进行预训练，然后在187条训练记录上进行微调以回答文本查询；利用传感器自身的校准仿射变换进行数据归一化（该步骤带来的精度提升超过所有架构改动之和）；无需训练分类头，模型直接嵌入记忆层进行开放查询。

Result: STAG基准（27个物体，未见过的记录）上top-1达到0.771±0.062（4次运行），top-3为0.935，超越监督闭集CNN的0.76。错误集中在少数接触歧义类别，与文本目标几何无关（Spearman rho≤0.05，702个类别对），对改写甚至裸命名查询保持稳定（1个点以内），两帧即可恢复八帧精度的89%。同时报道了失败案例：跨传感器预训练无增益、视觉协同训练损害触觉性能、错误归一化管线静默丢弃97%动态范围。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了电阻式压力阵列能够支撑强大的触觉文本查询模型，且小数据配方和传感器校准仿射是核心要素。模型权重、代码和记忆层全部开源，同时以同等精度报告正面与负面结果，为触觉表征学习提供了透明、可复现的开放基线。

Abstract: Resistive pressure arrays are the cheapest and most widely shipped tactile sensors, yet tactile representation learning has concentrated on optical sensors that image a deforming gel. We present Tactus, an open model that answers text queries from pressure data alone: on the STAG benchmark (27 objects, held-out recordings), it reaches 0.771 +/- 0.062 top-1 over four runs (top-3 0.935), matching, and at best exceeding, the dataset's supervised closed-set CNN at 0.76, with no trained classifier head. The recipe is small-data: 187 training recordings, masked-autoencoder pretraining on 144k unlabeled same-sensor frames, and the sensor's own calibration affine, which recovered more accuracy than every architecture change combined. The released model's errors concentrate in a few contact-ambiguous classes, are uncorrelated with text-target geometry (Spearman rho <= 0.05 over 702 class pairs), and survive paraphrased and even bare-name queries within one point; two diverse frames recover 89% of eight-frame accuracy. Failures are reported with equal precision: cross-sensor pretraining pooling gave no gain, vision co-training degraded touch, and a mis-normalized input pipeline silently discarded 97% of the sensor's dynamic range while producing plausible intermediate results. Weights, code, and the memory layer the model plugs into are released openly.

</details>


### [138] [Robust and Personalized Federated Learning for Aircraft-Engine Prognostics under Benign and Adversarial Client Heterogeneity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04045)
*Chinmoy Mitra,Md. Mehedi Hasan Nipu,Mohammad Sakib Mahmood,Md. Rakibul Islam,M. F. Mridha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本论文针对联邦学习（FL）在飞机发动机剩余使用寿命（RUL）预测中的两大挑战——良性异构性（不同运营商面临不同运行条件和故障模式）与对抗性异构性（被攻陷运营商提交恶意更新）进行了系统性研究。基于C-MAPSS基准数据集和多任务一维CNN模型，研究比较了三种良性异构性缓解方法、五种攻击与四种聚合方法，发现共享表征个性化可缩小约70%的本地到集中式的RMSE差距，而物理动机注入的传感器值后门攻击对标准平均聚合达到94.9%的攻击成功率，仅Krum聚合能抵御协调攻击者，个性化与鲁棒聚合结合可在保持小精度损失的同时将攻击成功率降至2.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习使机队运营商能在不共享原始数据的情况下联合训练剩余使用寿命预测模型，但面临两类关键挑战：良性异构性（诚实的运营商面对不同运行条件和故障模式）和对抗性异构性（被攻陷的运营商注入恶意更新）。现有研究缺乏对这些挑战的系统性、安全导向的评估，尤其是在航空安全关键背景下，需要全面衡量各种缓解策略和攻击方案的有效性。

Method: 采用多任务一维卷积神经网络，在商用模块化航空推进系统仿真（C-MAPSS）基准上构建结构性非IID数据划分，模拟机队运营商之间真实的良性数据异构。研究中比较了三种缓解良性异构性的方法（共享表征个性化、近端正则化、服务器端重加权），同时设计并评估了五种攻击方案（包括一种物理动机的传感器值后门攻击，用于掩盖发动机退化），针对四种聚合方法（标准平均、Krum等）进行对抗性测试。

Result: 共享表征个性化可弥合约70%的本地到集中式的RMSE差距，远优于近端正则化（21%）和服务器端重加权（10%）。传感器值后门攻击对标准平均聚合达到94.9%的攻击成功率且不显着影响干净精度，证明仅靠精度无法认证模型安全性。Krum将攻击成功率降低一个数量级，是唯一能抵御协调攻击者的聚合方法。个性化与鲁棒聚合相结合，可将攻击成功率降至2.8%，仅带来轻微精度代价。结果在客户端数量和更难的六条件数据集上保持一致。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了鲁棒更新选择与协作表征学习之间存在权衡：个性化本身不提供对抗保护，但将个性化与鲁棒聚合相结合可以同时获得两者优势。论文强调在安全关键的航空应用中，攻击成功率必须作为独立的评估指标显式衡量，不能仅依赖干净精度。代码和数据划分已公开以确保可复现性。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) enables aircraft fleet operators to jointly train remaining-useful-life (RUL) models from engine sensor telemetry without sharing raw data. This study examines two complementary challenges: benign heterogeneity, where honest operators observe different operating conditions and fault modes, and adversarial heterogeneity, where compromised operators submit poisoned updates. We conduct a controlled, safety-oriented evaluation using a multi-task one-dimensional convolutional neural network and a structurally non-IID partition of the Commercial Modular Aero-Propulsion System Simulation (C-MAPSS) benchmark. We compare four remedies for benign heterogeneity and evaluate five attacks against four aggregation methods, including a physically motivated sensor-value backdoor designed to mask engine degradation.
  Shared-representation personalization closes approximately 70% of the local-to-centralized root-mean-square-error gap, compared with 21% for proximal regularization and 10% for server-side reweighting. The backdoor achieves a 94.9% attack success rate against standard averaging while leaving clean accuracy statistically unchanged, demonstrating that accuracy alone cannot certify model safety and that attack success must be evaluated explicitly. Krum reduces attack success by an order of magnitude and is the only evaluated aggregator that withstands coordinated attackers, whereas personalization alone provides no protection. Combining personalization with robust aggregation restores robustness (2.8% attack success) with only a small accuracy cost, revealing a trade-off between robust update selection and collaborative representation learning. Results remain consistent across client counts and on a harder six-condition dataset. Code and data partitions are released for reproducibility.

</details>


### [139] [Recurrent Residual Quantization: A Progressive Multi-Precision Representation for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04048)
*Yu Luo,Bo Dong,Wenhua Cheng,Haihao Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出递归残差量化（RRQ）框架，通过低比特基座加一系列量化残差校正，从单一检查点支持多种有效精度（2/4/6/8位），无需校准数据且避免联合多比特优化，构建速度比MatGPTQ快3.3倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统量化方法对每个目标位宽都需要单独的训练/存储检查点，无法在推理时灵活适配不同的部署约束（精度、内存、吞吐量的权衡）。需要一种能够从单一检查点生成多种精度表示的方案。

Method: RRQ首先通过PTQ或RTN获得2位量化基座，然后逐步添加通过RTN生成的轻量2位残差来构建4、6、8位表示。方法是免校准的（calibration-free），且避免了多比特联合优化。整个2/4/6/8位全RTN包在Qwen3-8B上构建只需1293秒。

Result: 在六个近期LLM上的实验表明，RRQ在6位和8位精度下具有竞争力的准确率，4位精度表现依赖于具体模型。构建速度比MatGPTQ快3.3倍。

Conclusion: RRQ提供了一种高效且灵活的PTQ方案，可以从单一检查点获得多种位宽表示，在保持竞争力的同时大幅提升构建效率，为多样化的部署约束提供了实用选择。

Abstract: Serving large language models (LLMs) under diverse deployment constraints requires flexible trade-offs between accuracy, memory footprint, and throughput. However, conventional quantization methods typically require a separate checkpoint for each target bit-width. We introduce Recurrent Residual Quantization (RRQ), a post-training quantization (PTQ) framework that represents weights as a low-bit quantized base together with a sequence of quantized residual corrections, enabling multiple effective precisions from a single checkpoint. Starting from a 2-bit model obtained via post-training quantization (PTQ) or round-to-nearest (RTN), RRQ progressively adds lightweight 2-bit residuals generated via RTN to construct 4-, 6-, and 8-bit representations. The method is calibration-free and avoids joint multi-bit optimization. In our Qwen3-8B setup, the full all-RTN 2-/4-/6-/8-bit package is constructed in 1,293 seconds, 3.3 times faster than the measured MatGPTQ construction. Experiments on six recent LLMs show competitive accuracy at 6 and 8 bits, with model-dependent behavior at 4 bits. The code will be made publicly available upon publication.

</details>


### [140] [CAMP: A Cycle-Aware Multi-Scale Patch Mixer for Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04051)
*Jung Min Choi,Vijaya Krishna yalavarthi,Lars Schmidt-Thieme*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CAMP是一个周期感知的多尺度Patch混合模型，通过自适应周期学习、位置相关的Patch混合和去周期残差的多分辨率建模，在七个长期预测基准上取得最优或并列最优结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界时间序列常受重复模式支配，但主导周期可能在不同数据集、预测设置和输入窗口中变化。现有周期感知预测器通常在数据集级别选择单一周期，这在周期随时间变化或存在多个周期共存时过于局限。此外，基于Patch的模型对所有Patch位置统一处理，忽略了距离预测边界较远的Patch需要更广泛的上下文提炼，而近期Patch应更直接保留信息；去周期后的残差也跨越多个时间分辨率，单一尺度无法充分描述。

Method: CAMP包含三个核心模块：(1) 自适应周期学习模块，为每个输入窗口分别识别主导频率，无需预定义周期长度即可生成历史和未来周期分量；(2) 水平引导的Patch混合器，引入位置相关的细化机制，允许靠前的Patch融入更广泛的时间上下文，同时保留靠近预测边界的信息；(3) 通过时间对齐的多分辨率表示对去周期残差建模，在单一预测框架内捕获不同尺度的互补动态。

Result: 在七个长期预测基准上，CAMP在六个数据集上取得最佳平均MSE，在六个数据集上取得最佳或并列最佳MAE；在四个PEMS交通基准的十六个设置中取得最高MSE胜次数。

Conclusion: CAMP通过窗口级自适应周期学习、位置感知的Patch混合以及多尺度残差建模，有效解决了现有周期感知预测器在周期选择、Patch处理和残差建模方面的局限，在多个长时预测基准上展现出优越性能。

Abstract: Real-world time series are often governed by recurring patterns, but their dominant periods may vary across datasets, forecasting settings, and individual input windows. Existing cycle-aware forecasters commonly rely on a single period selected at the dataset level, which can be restrictive when periodic behavior changes over time or when multiple cycles coexist. Moreover, patch-based models typically process all patch positions uni- formly, although patches farther from the forecast boundary may require broader contextual refinement, while recent patches contain information that should be preserved more directly. Af- ter cyclic behavior is removed, the remaining dynamics may also span multiple temporal resolutions and cannot be adequately de- scribed at a single scale. We introduce CAMP, a Cycle-Aware Multi-Scale Patch Mixer designed to address these challenges. The Adaptive Cycle Learning module identifies dominant fre- quencies separately for each input window and generates both historical and future cyclic components without requiring a pre- defined cycle length. The Horizon-Guided Patch Mixer intro- duces position-dependent refinement, allowing earlier patches to incorporate broader temporal context while preserving infor- mation close to the forecast boundary. CAMP further models the de-cycled residual through temporally aligned multi-resolution representations, enabling complementary dynamics at different scales to be captured within one forecasting framework. Across seven long-term forecasting benchmarks, CAMP achieves the best average MSE on six datasets and the best or tied-best MAE on six. It also obtains the highest MSE win count across sixteen settings on four PEMS traffic benchmarks.

</details>


### [141] [LaPrune: Controllable Differentiable Sparsity at Million Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04057)
*Jakub Antczak,Joanna Wojciechowicz,Łukasz Struski,Jacek Tabor*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LaPrune，一种具有精确预算约束的可微分稀疏化层，通过LapSum屏障保持选择质量，并利用归一化二阶矩约束在给定预算下将掩码从均匀质量分配推向硬top-k选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Top-k选择决定了稀疏模型中哪些组件保持激活，但硬选择会阻断梯度传播，而连续松弛方法往往将掩码的"硬度"与所选质量耦合在一起，难以独立控制二者。

Method: LaPrune通过LapSum屏障保持选择质量不变，同时引入归一化二阶矩约束，使掩码在给定预算下从密集等质量分配逐步演化为硬top-k分配。推导了饱和分数的总体预测、近二值极限律以及近零分数的紧最坏情况界。

Result: 归一化硬度参数对分数尺度具有不变性，而固定LapSum温度则不具备这一性质；同时提供了饱和分数预测、近二值极限律和近零分数的紧最坏情况保证。

Conclusion: LaPrune提供了一种在保持精确预算的同时实现可微分硬top-k选择的方案，其归一化硬度参数具有尺度不变性，优于固定温度方法。

Abstract: Top-$k$ selection determines which components of a sparse model remain active. Hard selection blocks gradients, while continuous relaxations often couple mask hardness to the selected mass. We introduce LaPrune, a mathematically exact-budget differentiable layer that controls the normalized second moment while preserving the selected mass. A LapSum barrier preserves the selection mass, and a normalized second-moment constraint moves the mask from a dense equal-mass allocation toward hard top-$k$ at each budget. We derive a population prediction of the saturated fraction, a near-binary limiting law, and a tight worst-case guarantee on the near-zero fraction. The normalized hardness parameter is invariant to score scale, while a fixed LapSum temperature is not.

</details>


### [142] [SJEPA: Learning Elegant Latent Dynamics with Hybrid Symbolic-Neural Predictors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04060)
*Yongchao Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SJEPA是一个无重建的JEPA框架，通过将联合嵌入预测架构的过渡模型分解为符号规则与正则化神经修正的组合，学习具有紧凑符号描述的预测表征。其核心原则是学习最简单且充分的动力学，避免表征坍缩的同时获得符号化可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联合嵌入预测架构（JEPA）虽然能学习抽象状态，但其过渡模型通常是不可解释的黑箱神经映射。作者希望引入一种框架，使学习到的预测表征所诱导的动力学能够获得紧凑的符号化描述，从而在保持预测能力的同时提升可解释性。

Method: SJEPA采用混合过渡模型，将动力学分解为选定的符号文法（symbolic grammar）和针对符号外动力学的正则化神经修正两部分。框架通过两个关键机制实现"最简单且充分"的学习目标：(1) 表征约束保持信息丰富、非坍缩的预测坐标；(2) 算子压缩（operator compression）鼓励低复杂度的符号-神经混合过渡。此外，支持交替表征方程学习和针对固定表征的符号动力学拟合两种学习范式，并形式化分析了诱导动力学复杂度、预测坐标不可辨识性问题以及无约束算子压缩导致的表征坍缩捷径。

Result: 在受控摆实验中，联合学习相比事后拟合能发现显著更简单的符号动力学，同时获得更低的长期rollout误差和散度。无约束的一步诊断验证了预测的坍缩捷径。在文法误设定下，修正正则化能保留可表征的符号机制并将神经组件导向残差动力学。实验揭示了预测保真度、表征质量、符号简洁性和符号-神经分配之间的可控权衡。

Conclusion: SJEPA证明了JEPA框架可以在预测学习的同时获得符号化的动力学描述，联合学习优于事后拟合，且通过合理的正则化可以在预测能力与符号简洁性之间取得可控平衡。该工作为可解释的预测表征学习提供了新的方向。

Abstract: Joint-embedding predictive architectures learn abstract states by predicting target embeddings from context embeddings, but their transition models are typically opaque neural maps. We introduce SJEPA, a reconstruction-free JEPA framework that learns predictive representations whose induced dynamics admit compact symbolic descriptions. Its hybrid transition combines a symbolic law with a regularised neural correction for dynamics outside the selected grammar. The central principle is to learn the simplest adequate dynamics: representation constraints preserve informative, non-collapsed predictive coordinates, while operator compression favours low-complexity symbolic-neural transitions that remain predictively adequate. We formalise this principle through induced-dynamics complexity, analyse predictive-coordinate non-identifiability, and show that unconstrained operator compression creates a direct shortcut to representation collapse. The framework supports both alternating representation-equation learning and symbolic dynamics fitted to fixed representations. In controlled pendulum experiments, joint learning discovers substantially simpler symbolic dynamics with lower long-horizon rollout error and divergence than post-hoc fitting, while an unconstrained one-step diagnostic realises the predicted collapse shortcut. Under grammar misspecification, correction regularisation preserves the representable symbolic mechanism and directs the neural component towards residual dynamics. The results expose a controllable trade-off among predictive fidelity, representation quality, symbolic parsimony, and symbolic-neural allocation.

</details>


### [143] [Spend Bits Where Queries Look: KV Cache Vector Quantization with Attention-Preserving Transforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04074)
*Samuel Fernández-Menduiña,Amir Ziashahabi,Eduardo Pavez,Antonio Ortega,Salman Avestimehr*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出NOVA-KV，将KV缓存量化建模为变换编码问题，推导出注意力感知的最优非正交变换和等体积分组的等大小码本向量量化器，在每元素2比特下恢复大部分长上下文检索精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文LLM解码每一步都要读取KV缓存，加载开销超过注意力计算开销，系统受带宽限制。减少缓存大小可同时提升解码速度和服务容量，但现有方法要么是数据无关的变换，要么未从失真准则推导变换，且依赖随机或Hadamard旋转（只均衡方差不能压缩能量）和固定宽度标量量化器（低码率下次优）。

Method: 将KV缓存量化形式化为以注意力乘积误差为失真的变换编码问题；从校准统计量在高分辨率模型下推导键和值的闭式最优变换；证明最优键变换非正交且满足广义Parseval关系，使注意力感知失真等价于变换域的MSE；据此可用MSE最优向量量化器直接作用于变换后的键系数；通过将系数分组为等体积划分，使等大小码本在高分辨率模型下达到变长码率最优。

Result: 在每元素2比特下，NOVA-KV恢复了标量量化方法在可比吞吐量下丢失的大部分长上下文检索精度。

Conclusion: 基于失真准则推导的注意力感知最优变换结合等体积分组量化，能在极低码率下有效保护注意力乘积，显著优于现有基于随机/哈达玛旋转的标量量化方法。

Abstract: Long-context LLM decoding reads the key-value (KV) cache at every step. Loading it takes longer than computing attention over it, so throughput is bandwidth-bound. Hence, reducing the cache size can raise both decoding speed and serving capacity. The challenge is to reduce cache size while preserving the attention products, keeping reconstruction cheap, and using a fixed per-token bit count. At two bits per element, the most competitive methods rely on orthogonal transforms. However, existing techniques are either data-oblivious or use the query statistics without deriving the transform from a distortion criterion. Moreover, they rely on transforms built on top of random or Hadamard rotations, which equalize variances across entries rather than compacting energy, and fixed-width scalar quantizers, which are suboptimal at low rates. In this paper, we formulate KV cache quantization as a transform coding problem in which distortion is the error in the attention products. We derive closed-form optimal transforms for keys and values from calibration statistics, under a high-resolution model. We show that the optimal key transform is not orthogonal and satisfies a generalized Parseval relation: the attention-aware distortion becomes mean-squared error (MSE) in the transform domain. Thus, we can use MSE-optimal vector quantizers applied directly to the transformed key coefficients. To meet the fixed-width layout requirement, we show that grouping coefficients into equal-volume partitions makes equal-size codebooks attain the variable-rate optimum under the same high-resolution model. At two bits per element, our method, termed NOVA-KV, recovers most of the long-context retrieval accuracy lost by scalar quantization methods at comparable throughput.

</details>


### [144] [Local Violation Certification for Linear Predict-Then-Optimize Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04474)
*Ş. İlker Birbil,Wenhao Chi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对线性决策管线在输入不确定性下的局部违规认证问题，提出了一个直接结构化的框架，通过单次优化求解闭式计算出局部违规风险，避免了传统随机采样方法在罕见事件下的高计算成本，并提供特征级归因分析，在电力经济调度系统中验证了其高效性和可审计性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据驱动的决策管线（预测机器学习模型+下游优化）在高风险运营决策中日益普及，但传统情景生成方法依赖重复随机测试，在罕见违规事件发生时计算成本过高，且无法提供失败原因的深入洞察。需要一种更高效、更具解释性的认证框架。

Method: 本文提出一个专为线性决策管线设计的局部违规认证框架。核心思路是：首先从数学上论证标准采样方法对罕见违规场景效率低下，从而转向直接结构化方法；通过分析已部署管线的固定决策边界，利用单次优化求解闭式计算局部违规风险；同时引入精确采样过程和闭式风险统计量，提供特征级归因（识别哪些输入特征对潜在不合规贡献最大）。

Result: 在受排放法规约束的经济电力调度系统上进行验证，该方法能够提供精确、可审计的风险评估，计算成本仅为传统方法的极小比例，同时具备特征级归因能力。

Conclusion: 本文提出的直接结构化认证框架为线性决策管线的安全性验证提供了一种高效、精确且可解释的替代方案，克服了传统随机采样方法在罕见违规事件场景下的计算瓶颈。

Abstract: Data-driven decision pipelines combining predictive machine learning models with downstream optimization software are increasingly used to make high-stakes operational decisions. Certifying the safety, fairness, and reliability of these decisions is essential, yet traditional scenario generation methods rely on repeated random testing, which becomes computationally prohibitive when failure events are rare and offers little insight into why failures occur. We present a framework for local violation certification designed specifically for linear decision pipelines under input uncertainty. We mathematically demonstrate that standard sampling methods fail efficiently for rare violations, motivating a direct structural approach. By analyzing the fixed decision boundary of a deployed pipeline, we show that the local risk of failure can be calculated directly in closed form using a single optimization solve. Furthermore, we introduce an exact sampling procedure and closed-form risk statistics that provide feature-level attributions (identifying which input characteristics contribute most to potential non-compliance) without requiring repetitive random trials or complex sampling algorithms. We demonstrate our approach on an economic power dispatch system subject to emissions regulations, delivering precise, auditable risk assessments at a fraction of the traditional computational cost.

</details>


### [145] [Spatiotemporal Graph Transformer for Traffic Intelligence in Edge Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04075)
*Laha Ale,Letian Lin,Na Cao,Zheng Ma,Peng Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种时空图Transformer框架，通过图神经网络捕捉边缘计算中服务区域间的空间相关性，利用Transformer自注意力机制学习交通流量的长程时间依赖，在真实蜂窝网络数据集上优于GCN-RNN、GCN-LSTM和GCN-GRU等基线模型，实现更有效的主动资源调配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘计算中的流量预测对主动资源管理至关重要，但实际蜂窝边缘系统中流量具有强空间相关性和长程时间依赖性。现有循环预测方法虽能捕捉短期动态，但在非平稳条件下难以建模长期流量演化，亟需一种能同时建模空间交互与长程时间依赖的预测框架。

Method: 提出时空图Transformer框架，将空间与时间建模解耦：使用图神经网络（GNN）捕捉服务区域间的空间相关性，利用基于自注意力的Transformer学习历史流量观测中的长程时间模式，实现大规模时空流量建模。

Result: 在真实蜂窝网络数据集上的大量实验表明，提出的图Transformer在多个预测时域上一致优于基于循环图网络的基线模型（GCN-RNN、GCN-LSTM、GCN-GRU），生成的预测能实现更有效的主动资源供应并降低过载风险。

Conclusion: 图增强的注意力机制在构建智能、自适应的边缘计算系统方面具有显著潜力，所提框架为大规模时空流量预测提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Accurate traffic forecasting is essential for proactive resource management in edge computing, where service demand evolves dynamically across both space and time. In practical cellular edge systems, traffic exhibits strong spatial correlations among neighboring service regions and long-range temporal dependencies driven by user mobility and application behavior. Existing recurrent forecasting approaches can capture short-term dynamics but often struggle to model long-horizon traffic evolution under non-stationary conditions. To address this challenge, we propose a spatiotemporal graph Transformer framework that jointly models spatial interactions and temporal dependencies for traffic forecasting in edge computing. The framework employs graph neural networks to capture spatial correlations among service regions and leverages Transformer-based self-attention to learn long-range temporal patterns from historical traffic observations. By decoupling spatial representation learning from temporal reasoning, the proposed approach provides an effective mechanism for large-scale spatiotemporal traffic modeling. Extensive experiments on a real-world cellular network dataset demonstrate that the proposed graph Transformer consistently outperforms recurrent graph-based baselines, including GCN-RNN, GCN-LSTM, and GCN-GRU models, across multiple forecasting horizons. The resulting forecasts enable more effective proactive resource provisioning and reduce overload risk compared with reactive management strategies. These results highlight the potential of graph-enhanced attention mechanisms for building intelligent and adaptive edge computing systems.

</details>


### [146] [SpecDrop: Parameter-Free Category-Conditioned Routing for Modular Specialization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04084)
*Boyao Wang,Zhihan Lei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SpecDrop，一种无参数、无需学习的固定路由方案：每个分支对指定类别分配权重p_a，对其他类别有少量泄漏p_i，通过类别无关的固定分母合并，推断时需提供类别标签。实验表明在单超类标签的视觉任务（CIFAR-100、ImageNet-1K）上SpecDrop显著超过参数匹配但不用标签的基线，但给定相同标签时直接对稠密模型输出做掩码在纯准确率上更强；关键贡献是将标签转化为训练好的模块化结构（分支-类别对齐58%/100%），且掩码增益大量内化。在模糊划分任务（SlimPajama-6B语言建模、SuperNI指令微调）中则退化为与No-Routing对照无差异。作者结论：决定路由是否有效的是训练信号粒度与目标类别的对齐程度，而非路由算法本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在总参数预算匹配时，学习到的路由（learned routers）可能不如等权重的No-Routing基线。作者希望厘清瓶颈究竟在于路由算法本身，还是在于训练信号粒度与目标类别之间的对齐关系，从而确定路由机制真正发挥作用的条件。

Method: SpecDrop是一种固定的参数无关路由方案：K个分支中每个分支对其指定类别获得权重p_a，对其他类别有小的泄漏p_i>0，通过一个类别无关的固定分母合并。无学习路由参数、无辅助损失，推断时需类别标签。在标签清晰的视觉任务（CIFAR-100/ResNet-110、ImageNet-1K/ViT-S16）和模糊划分任务（SlimPajama-6B/30M Transformer语言建模、SuperNI/Llama-3.2-1B LoRA指令微调）上对比。

Result: 视觉任务上SpecDrop达79.23%（CIFAR-100）和79.89%（ImageNet-1K），超过不用标签的参数匹配基线（CIFAR-100比稠密高4.75，ImageNet-1K比No-Routing+SE高6.53）；但给定相同标签时，对稠密模型输出做掩码在纯准确率上更高（85.2/83.7）。实现了58%/100%分支-类别对齐，掩码增益为0.00（CIFAR）/+1.06（ImageNet），表明输出空间限制被大量内化。模糊划分任务上路由机制与No-Routing对照在种子噪声内无差异。

Conclusion: 决定路由是否有益的是训练信号粒度与目标类别的对齐程度（granularity alignment），而非路由算法的选择。将类别标签转化为训练期内的模块化结构是SpecDrop式路由的核心价值。

Abstract: Mixture-of-experts (MoE) networks pursue specialization through learned routers, gates, and load-balancing losses, yet at matched total-parameter budgets learned routers can underperform equal-weight No-Routing baselines. Is the bottleneck the routing algorithm, or the alignment between training-signal granularity and the target categories? We probe the question with SpecDrop, a fixed parameter-free routing scheme: each of $K$ branches receives weight $p_a$ for its assigned category and a small leakage $p_i > 0$ otherwise, merged through a category-independent fixed denominator, with no learned routing parameters and no auxiliary losses; the category label is required at inference. On vision tasks where each image has one superclass label (CIFAR-100 on ResNet-110; ImageNet-1K on ViT-S/16), SpecDrop reaches 79.23% on CIFAR-100 and 79.89% on ImageNet-1K, exceeding parameter-matched baselines that do not use the label (+4.75 over dense on CIFAR-100; +6.53 over the No-Routing+SE control on ImageNet-1K). These gains quantify what category supervision buys when deployed through routing -- not an advantage over label-aware deployments of the baselines: given the same label, masking a dense model's outputs is stronger for accuracy alone (85.2 / 83.7). SpecDrop's contribution is converting the label into trained-in modular structure: 58%/100% branch-category alignment, and masking gains of 0.00 (CIFAR) / +1.06 (ImageNet) -- the output-space restriction is largely internalized during training.
